Source presence does not prove exactness, but once independent current-target ownership proves authored game code, exact mismatch must not push the function back out of the authored denominator.

Target keeps an entry `push ecx` four-byte frame, but IDA exposes no local variable in that frame; truthful `float glyph_width_value`, `int glyph_height`, and `unsigned char mode_value` A/B probes all compile byte-identically to the 102-byte baseline. - `gpt-web-fighter-command-flags-runtime` now formally replays `match_command @ 0x00463500` at **1006/960**, canonical through +0x8A, while exact builder `0x00476B10` remains 497/497. The matcher calls checked-deque dereference helper `0x00428570` five times; that helper is independently compiler-generated by the fixed-slot COMDAT origin rule, so the formal relocation contract cleanly separates authored command policy from generated STL machinery.
- Conservative progress after this denominator correction is **1,272 / 1,385 authored functions exact (91.8412%)** and **215,400 / 2,073,220 authored bytes exact (10.3896%)**, with **1,299 source-present** mappings. The lower percentages are evidence-quality progress, not a reconstruction regression.

## 2026-09-08 roster Object shared-primitive and missed-tail candidate wave

- Five current-target authored roots are newly canonical exact, **662 bytes** total: `SecondaryAnimationRenderRuntimeView_set_vertex_color @ 0x0042FD80` (52), `CharacterObjectEffectEmitter_set_secondary_animation_alpha @ 0x00496370` (35), `CharacterObjectEffectEmitter_release_secondary_animation_renderer @ 0x004963F0` (43), `CharacterObjectEffectEmitter_update_ping_pong_motion_and_periodic_spawn @ 0x0053CBC0` (450), and `CharacterObjectEffectEmitter_position_relative_to_owner_offsets @ 0x005B0400` (82). All five replay from ordinary pinned VC8 C++; no assembly, copied bytes, padding, fake liveness, volatile/register forcing, or ABI lies are used.
- `0x0042FD80` is a genuine **IDA-missed callable candidate**. Raw PE has eight INT3 bytes immediately before it, a complete 52-byte body ending in `ret 4`, and INT3 padding immediately after. IDA instead reports the address as a remote tail chunk of `sub_496370`; its only direct xref is the `0x0049638B` tail jump. A separate natural C++ method emits exactly the 52 target bytes, so the provisional inventory expands **4010 -> 4011** rather than trusting the IDA function list as complete.
- `0x00496370` closes the other side of that tail relationship. Ten current direct callers lie in RTTI-owned roster Object `+0x28` giants. The wrapper guards renderer `+0x338`, zero-extends its alpha byte, forms `(alpha << 24) | 0x00FFFFFF`, and naturally tail-calls exact `0x0042FD80`; pinned VC8 reproduces all 35 bytes. This is independent authored call-graph evidence for the wrapper, not a split-byte accounting trick.
- The three other CharacterObjectEffectEmitter roots are shared semantic bricks under the Object giants. `0x0053CBC0` has one direct caller from every roster Object `+0x28` root and is exact only when the direction-one phase decay is expressed as the target cold-expire `>=4` arm. `0x004963F0` is ordinary secondary-renderer delete/clear ownership. `0x005B0400` copies owner facing and publishes owner-relative integer offsets; its callers span eleven roster Object giant families.
- This wave intentionally grows both denominator and numerator. Current tracking is **1,264 exact / 1,362 confirmed authored functions**, **214,705 exact / 2,062,096 confirmed authored bytes**, and **1,384 review candidates**. Small exact primitives are not sufficient for the 99% byte objective, but they reduce the unknown call graph inside the 25--80KB Object roots and provide fixed semantics for reconstructing those roots next.
- Next shared lead is `0x00496420` (274 bytes), the high-fanout secondary-animation replacement/initializer. Target evidence already fixes delete/new of the exact 0x54 renderer, short-normalized lookup in the animation tree at `+0x160`, checked first-frame selection, and exact initializer `0x00430750`. A private whole-TU probe was deliberately rejected as canonical evidence because including the renderer implementation pulled local deque `_Tidy` COMDAT relocations into the probe. Recover it through a clean shared type/header boundary rather than weakening relocation checks.

## Cross-version seed decision

The former repository targeted 1.06 (`3,039,232` bytes), not 1.06a, so its
addresses, classifications, and exact claims remain invalid for the current
target. A raw-byte uniqueness survey may be used only to prioritize retained
source hypotheses.

The first migration wave intersected old authored/exact hypotheses with unique
raw-identical 1.06a candidates, then rebuilt every retained source with the
pinned VC8 toolchain and required a fresh canonical zero-difference compare.
That procedure promoted 47 functions; raw identity by itself promoted none.
REL32 destinations were checked against the current target. In particular, the
second loader called by `load_spell_data` is `0x00434300` in 1.06a; a guessed
cross-version delta produced a mismatch and was rejected before IDA confirmed
the current call target.

## Next bounded work

Continue origin/boundary review so the authored denominator becomes meaningful,
then expand exact recovery from the 690 accepted functions. Use
`scripts/rank_retained_exact.py --only-unconfigured` to prioritize historical
exact source that never had an old match unit. Same-size zero non-relocation
mismatch candidates are especially productive, but ambiguous template/clone
families must be disambiguated with
current xrefs/vtables/RTTI/relocations. Treat every old 1.06 address, callee,
name, and implementation as a hypothesis until independently reconciled
against 1.06a.

The 95% authored-function and authored-byte goals cannot be reported yet: 2,247
provisional candidates still need authored/excluded classification at this committed checkpoint, so the
global authored denominator is not established. Do not use the current exact subset as a substitute denominator.
